    'function optionalLocation(): Promise<{ latitude: number | null; longitude: number | null }> {\n' +
    '  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n' +
    '    const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(null, null), 8000);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    function finish(latitude: number | null, longitude: number | null) {\n' +
    '      clearTimeout(timer);\n' +
    '      resolve({ latitude, longitude });\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    if (!navigator.geolocation) return finish(null, null);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '    try {\n' +
    '      navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(\n' +
    '        (position) => finish(position.coords.latitude, position.coords.longitude),\n' +
    '        () => finish(null, null),\n' +
    '        { enableHighAccuracy: false, timeout: 7000, maximumAge: 0 }\n' +
    '      );\n' +
    '    } catch {\n' +
    '      finish(null, null);\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '  });\n' +
